### 2026-08-07 22:30:05  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Car Registrations #Importance 1

Brazil New Car Sales Hit Highest Since 2014 — Brazil's new vehicle sales rose 2.6% month-over-month to 279.5 thousand units in July 2026, rebounding from a 0.8% decline in June, marking the highest level since December 2014. Compared with a year earlier, sales jumped 14.9%, marking the strongest monthly performance of 2026 despite the school holiday period. Electrified vehicles continued to gain market share, accounting for 23.5% of total registrations, up from less than 11% a year earlier. Year-to-date sales of electrified models surged 120.8%, supported by record registrations of 25.8 thousand fully electric vehicles in July. In the commercial segment, truck sales continued to recover, aided by financing incentives under the Move Brasil program, while bus sales remained weak, down 10.7% so far this year due to delays in the Caminhos da Escola program. In the first seven months of 2026, total vehicle sales exceeded 1.7 million units, up 17.9% from a year earlier.

### 2026-08-07 22:27:32  #市場 #觀點 #央行 #國際

【美股早盤上漲 非農令加息預期降溫但通脹資料可能才是決定因素】美國就業市場急劇降溫，推動股市上漲、債券收益率下跌。市場猜測，美聯儲短期內或無需被迫加息。紐約時間9:33，標普500指數上漲0.4%，納斯達克100指數上漲1.1%，道指基本持平。股市暫停兩日下跌，標普500指數有望創4月以來最佳單週表現。eToro的Bret Kenwell表示，最新就業報告疲軟到足以減輕美聯儲加息壓力，但還沒有弱到表明勞動力市場或整體經濟正在失速。他說：“通脹仍然令人擔憂，但今天的資料可能給決策者更多理由保持耐心，也給投資者更多空間投入風險資產。”Wilsey Asset Management的Brent Wilsey表示，週五的就業報告不僅遠遜於預期，還顯示經濟在7月減少了就業崗位，這令美聯儲陷入兩難，因為通脹仍處於高位且具有黏性。高盛資產管理的Lindsay Rosner表示：“雖然接下來的通脹資料將起到最終決定作用，但就業增長放緩支援美聯儲9月維持利率不變。”摩根士丹利財富管理的Ellen Zentner指出，疲軟的非農就業資料可能減輕美聯儲9月會議加息的壓力，但下週公佈的通脹資料仍可能是決定性因素。她補充說：“如果通脹資料高於預期，那麼即使勞動力市場降溫，也可能不足以壓低美聯儲內部的加息呼聲，或降低市場對加息的預期。”

### 2026-08-07 22:26:58  #宏觀 #國際

【韓國加強單一股票槓桿ETF監管 產品成交量縮水至十分之一】韓國監管機構出臺針對單一股票槓桿型ETF的監管政策後，相關產品成交額大幅萎縮，資金流向成為投資者關注焦點。 市場也出現一種預期：此前扎堆部分個股的短期資金迎來重新佈局，有望改善科斯達克（Kosdaq）市場的資金供需環境。 據證券行業7日訊息，7月單一股票槓桿ETF日均成交額高達12.3萬億韓元；監管措施自7月31日落地後，成交額驟降至約3.2萬億韓元。近3個交易日日均成交額進一步回落至1萬億韓元左右，交易規模較鼎盛時期縮水至十分之一。 新韓投資證券研究員樸宇烈分析：“高槓杆頭寸集中平倉，疊加監管重拳落地，8月以來單一股票槓桿ETF交易額急劇下滑。” 資金交易格局也發生明顯轉變。韓國交易所與科斯康ETF資料平臺（ETF CHECK）統計顯示，自上月31日至前一交易日，KODEX 200穩居ETF成交額榜首。

### 2026-08-07 22:25:40  #央行 #國際

【美國個人投資者協會調查：散戶樂觀情緒突然升溫，但仍低於歷史平均水平】根據美國個人投資者協會（AAII）最新一期周度調查，越來越多的散戶投資者看好未來六個月股市表現，但樂觀比例仍低於歷史平均水平，這已是過去四周內第三次出現該情況。 看漲投資者比例從上週的31%升至37%，接近但略低於37.5%的歷史平均水平。看跌投資者比例從上週的42.2%下降至38%，但已連續第六個月（26周）高於31.5%的歷史平均水平。持中性觀點的投資者比例從26.9%降至25%，這是過去23週中第22次低於31.0%的歷史平均水平。 AAII本週針對利率政策的特別調查顯示，主街投資者並未普遍呼籲美聯儲降息。55.1%的受訪者認為，美聯儲上週維持隔夜貸款利率不變的決定是正確的；30.6%的投資者認為美聯儲應該加息。 相比之下，認為美聯儲應該降息的投資者比例僅為5.6%，低於表示“不確定或沒有看法”的8.7%。

### 2026-08-07 22:19:21  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Business Confidence #Importance 1

Canada Ivey PMI Signals Slower Expansion — Canada's Ivey PMI fell to 55.1 in July 2026 from 56.2 in June, slightly below market expectations of 55.5, but remained in expansionary territory. The reading marked the second consecutive monthly decline and the lowest level since March. The employment index eased to 52.2 from 53.6, although it continued to signal job growth. Meanwhile, the prices index accelerated to 75.5 from 73.7, indicating stronger cost pressures. The supplier deliveries index rose to 45.9 from 44.3 after remaining broadly stable around 44 since April, while the inventories index increased to 54.9 from 50.9, staying in expansionary territory.

### 2026-08-07 22:14:37  #Trading Economics #Markets #Canada #Stock Market #Importance 1

TSX Hits Record High on Mining Rally — The S&P/TSX Composite Index rose over 0.5% to trade above 36,000 on Friday, reaching a fresh record as mining stocks rallied. Gold prices surged as weaker US labor data and lower energy prices reduced expectations of a Federal Reserve rate hike this year. The US economy unexpectedly shed jobs in July, while oil prices wavered amid uncertainty over a potential US-Iran deal to restore exports through the Strait of Hormuz. Agnico Eagle and WPM jumped over 7%, while Barrick gained 5.5% and other gold miners rose between 4% and 10%. In contrast, banking and broader sectors weakened as strong Canadian employment data raised the prospect of a BoC rate hike if energy prices remain elevated. Canada's unemployment rate fell 0.1 percentage point to 6.4%, slightly below forecasts of 6.5%, while employment increased by 75,000, well above expectations for a 15,000 gain. RBC and TD Bank both shed about 0.5%.

### 2026-08-07 22:13:10  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Silver Rises to 7-Week High — Silver price rose to over $63.5 per ounce on Friday, the highest in seven weeks, as markets reconsidered the likelihood that the Federal Reserve will hike rates this year. New jobs data showed an unexpected drop in payrolls, limiting the leeway for higher rates to attend inflationary pressures. Still, fuel and natural gas prices held most of their pullback in August as the US presidential administration chased an agreement with Iran to restore energy exports through the Persian Gulf. Lower risks of a rate lowered yields and benefited precious metals. In turn, industrial demand for silver also supported bids, as Chinese imports of silver-bearing ores surged 62.5% annually in June to 219,000 tonnes. The data was in line with an expanding production for solar panels and electricity grids. Still, lingering risks of a flare-up in energy prices maintained silver relatively close to the seven-month low of $55 per ounce from July 16th.

【媒體：加拿大正與美國商討貿易方面的讓步，以換取部分關稅減免】據報道，隨著美國威脅實施新一輪關稅前談判日益白熱化，加拿大和美國正在商討一項潛在協議：渥太華將同意滿足特朗普政府提出的一系列貿易要求，以換取部分行業關稅的減免。據三位熟悉談判情況的行業訊息人士透露，雙方已就多項提議進行了深入討論，並交換了書面談判立場，但目前尚未達成協議。目前討論方案的核心是一項“交換”：加拿大將在一系列美國認為是雙邊貿易主要矛盾的問題上作出讓步，包括取消針對美國商品（如汽車）的報復性關稅；恢復美國酒類產品進入加拿大市場；取消各省政府採購限制；以及接受華盛頓對於乳製品配額分配方式的解釋。其中一名訊息人士表示，美國方面列出了一份優先事項清單，大約包含10項希望加拿大解決的問題。作為交換，美國將降低針對鋼鐵和鋁產品徵收的行業關稅，即所謂的“232條款”。加拿大方面還希望美國降低汽車和林產品關稅。不過訊息人士稱，美國預計不會完全取消232條款的關稅，而談判的關鍵部分圍繞著將保留的關稅水平展開。

### 2026-08-07 22:01:57  #MKT News #Important

【Globe and Mail: Canada negotiating US trade concessions for partial tariff relief】Canada and the United States are negotiating a potential deal in which Ottawa would meet a series of Trump administration trade demands in exchange for partial rollbacks of tariffs, the Globe and Mail reports. Insiders say written positions have been exchanged and about 10 US priority items are under discussion. Proposed Canadian concessions include removing retaliatory tariffs on US autos, restoring access for US spirits, lifting provincial procurement restrictions, and accepting Washington’s interpretation of dairy quota allocations. In return Washington would reduce — but is not expected to fully repeal — Section 232 tariffs on steel and aluminum; Canada is also seeking lower tariffs on autos and forest products. No agreement has been reached.

### 2026-08-07 22:01:57  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Africa #Currency #Importance 0

South African Rand at 1-Month High — The South African rand traded around 16.2 per US dollar, its highest since early July, mainly supported by firmer prices of key precious metals, particularly gold, which benefited from reduced expectations of Federal Reserve rate hikes. Meanwhile, investors monitored negotiations involving the US, Iran and Oman over the Strait of Hormuz amid persistent geopolitical tensions. The rand hit a three-month low against the dollar last month after the SARB unexpectedly held rates steady on July 23, despite warning of rising inflation risks.The currency had already been affected by elevated oil prices after the Iran conflict erupted, given South Africa's status as a net energy importer. Still, investors see longer-term support from a stronger economy, healthy public finances, and the central bank's commitment to its 3% inflation target. Headline and core inflation rose to 5.0% and 4.1% in June, respectively, remaining above the SARB's target range.

Ibovespa Edges Lower on Corporate Results — The Ibovespa edged lower to hover near 175,000 on Friday amid another batch of 2Q26 earnings reports. Localiza edged lower despite reporting R$1 billion in net profit, up 30.6% from a year earlier. Renner slumped 10% after cutting its 2026 net revenue guidance as sales slowed sharply during the World Cup period. Magazine Luiza also fell nearly 5% after reporting a net loss in 2Q26 almost three times larger than in 2Q25. BB Seguridade dropped 6% after its dividend cutoff date passed on Thursday, with shares now trading ex-dividend. Bradesco BBI said the company is likely to continue facing a challenging environment after its pension business disappointed, reflecting weaker financial results. In contrast, Petrobras gained over 0.5% after beating expectations, as supply disruptions linked to the US-Iran conflict lifted second-quarter prices for crude, gasoline, and diesel. Its EBITDA rose 80% year-on-year.

【租房補貼每月最高5000元 海口人才住房保障新規下月施行】近日，記者從海口市住房和城鄉建設局獲悉，海口市人民政府修訂印發新版《海口市引進人才住房保障實施細則》，該《細則》自2026年9月1日起施行。新規最佳化人才住房保障覆蓋範圍、補貼標準與辦理流程，全方位完善引進人才安居支援政策。《細則》明確，申請購房補貼的所購房屋須為2018年5月13日後新購買的住房（含商品住宅、產權式酒店、酒店式公寓、共有產權住房、限售商品住房），購房時間以購房合同備案時間為準。2018年5月13日以後第一次在海口市就業或第一次在海口市自主創業且實際工作地在海口市的以下人才，適用《細則》並納入相應的住房保障：屬於海南自由貿易港高層次人才中的A、B、C三類人才且符合本省、市規定條件的；50歲以下且屬於海南自由貿易港高層次人才中的D、E兩類人才，急需緊缺人才可放寬至55歲（含外籍和港澳臺地區人才）；40歲以下全日制碩士畢業生及35歲以下全日制本科畢業生（含國外、境外高校畢業生）；35歲以下具有中級專業職稱、技師職業資格(二級職業技能等級)或具有國家和本省已明確規定可聘任中級專業技術職務的執業資格人才；總部經濟企業引進的高層管理人才；經省、市政府認定給予住房保障的其他人才。（海南日報）

S&P 500 Tests New Record — US stocks were mostly higher on Friday as soft jobs data dimmed risks of a rate hike by the Federal Reserve next month. The S&P 500 rose 0.3% to a record high, while Nasdaq 100 gained 0.9% and the Dow was flat. Non-farm payrolls unexpectedly fell by 23,000 from the previous month and wage growth fell, while both the unemployment and labor force participation rates fell, erasing concerns that a strong labor market was inflationary. Yields pulled back despite signals that the FOMC is more focused on the inflation side of the mandate, supporting credit sensitive equities amid the rebound for hyperscalers and banks this week. SpaceX extended yesterday's rebound even as $101 billion in its shares were unlocked from their post-IPO holds. Meanwhile, Sandisk and Western Digital gained 2% each following their earnings-spurred selloff yesterday, maintaining volatility in the chip-producing space. Also, Berkshire Hathaway shares were flat ahead of their earnings tomorrow.

【北京出臺房地產新政策 適度提高住房公積金最高貸款額度】8月7日，北京市住房和城鄉建設委員會、北京市規劃和自然資源委員會、北京住房公積金管理中心7日晚聯合印發《關於進一步最佳化調整本市房地產政策的通知》，適度提高住房公積金最高貸款額度。購房家庭中1人為公積金繳存人的，購買首套住房公積金貸款最高貸款額度為120萬元，二套住房公積金貸款最高額度為100萬元；夫妻雙方均為繳存人的，購買首套住房公積金貸款最高貸款額度為240萬元，二套住房公積金貸款最高額度為200萬元。符合以下條件的，最高貸款額度可進一步上浮：1.城六區戶籍居民家庭，在城六區外購買首套住房的，最高可上浮20萬元；2.購買住房符合本市建築綠色發展支援政策的，最高可上浮40萬元；3.本市戶籍二孩及以上多子女家庭購買住房的，可上浮40萬元。同時符合多項條件的，最高貸款額度可疊加上浮，購房家庭中1人為公積金繳存人的，最高上浮60萬元；夫妻雙方均為繳存人的，最高上浮100萬元。實際貸款額度依據購房家庭還款能力確定。

### 2026-08-07 21:32:52  #MKT News #Important #Market Regions #Greater China #CSI300 #Impact bullish #CNH

【Beijing raises housing provident fund loan caps; couples’ first-home HPF borrowing can reach 3.4 mln yuan】Beijing’s Housing and Urban‑Rural Development Commission, Municipal Planning and Natural Resources Commission and Housing Provident Fund Management Center on the evening of the 7th issued a notice raising maximum housing provident fund (HPF) loan limits. If only one household member contributes to HPF, the maximum HPF loan for a first home is 1.2 mln yuan and for a second home 1.0 mln yuan. If both spouses contribute, the caps are 2.4 mln yuan for a first home and 2.0 mln yuan for a second home. Eligibility for additional uplifts: (1) households with hukou in the city’s six central districts buying a first home outside those districts: +200k yuan; (2) purchases that qualify under Beijing’s green building support policy: +400k yuan; (3) Beijing hukou families with two or more children buying housing: +400k yuan. Multiple conditions may stack: maximum additional uplift is +600k yuan for one contributor and +1.0 mln yuan for both contributors, implying a potential first‑home cap of up to 1.8 mln yuan (one contributor) or 3.4 mln yuan (both contributors). Actual loan amounts remain subject to household repayment ability.

【Nick Timiraos: July payrolls give no clear signal; inflation key to Fed rate path】WSJ’s Nick Timiraos says July’s US jobs report will be hard to interpret. Evidence that the labour market has not re-accelerated could reduce urgency for a next-month hike, but outcomes hinge on fresher inflation data. A drop in unemployment will keep markets focused on price trends; whether price pressures rise or fall will determine if more officials conclude they cannot meet the inflation target with rates left unchanged. Two months of milder inflation would strengthen the case to hold rates as a trend signal rather than noise; stronger inflation would revive hawkish forecasts and could give officials favoring further tightening a path to secure a pivotal fourth vote.

### 2026-08-07 21:27:17  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Gold Rises to 2-Month High — Gold prices rose to above $4,350 per ounce on Friday, the highest in two months as lower energy prices and softening labor conditions limited expectations that the Federal Reserve would deliver a rate hike this year. The US economy unexpectedly shed jobs in July while wages and the labor force participation rate fell, erasing any views that the strong labor market was inflationary. On top of that, energy prices lost some of the traction from previous sessions despite lingering uncertainty of a deal between Iran and the US to restore exports through the Strait of Hormuz. Treasury yields consequently eased, lifting bullion prices as markets faced lower opportunity costs to hold assets that bear no interest. Elsewhere, data from clearing institutions indicated that institutional investors in China continued to increase long position on gold-backed assets for safety from volatility in tech stocks and recent signs of strength in the physical market, recently underpinned by central banks.

【北京：支援裝修提取住房公積金】為貫徹中共中央政治局會議關於穩定房地產市場的要求，更好滿足居民多樣化住房消費需求，立足首都功能定位，結合當前房地產市場形勢，北京市住房和城鄉建設委員會、北京市規劃和自然資源委員會、北京住房公積金管理中心聯合印發《關於進一步最佳化調整本市房地產政策的通知》（以下簡稱《通知》），自2026年8月8日起施行。《通知》包括最佳化住房限購政策、完善房屋贈與政策、加大住房公積金支援力度3個方面7項政策措施。按《通知》有關規定，本市行政區域內自住住房的產權人及其配偶，對自住住房進行裝修裝飾的，可申請提取住房公積金，根據裝修裝飾增值稅發票金額的50%計算提取額度，每套房屋最高不超過25萬元。同一產權人就同一住房再次申請裝修提取，距上次裝修提取應滿10年。

### 2026-08-07 21:21:37  #Trading Economics #Economy #Chile #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Chile Trade Surplus Widens Below Expectations — Chile's trade surplus widened to $1.99 billion in July 2026 from $297 million a year earlier, but fell short of forecasts for a $2.38 billion surplus. Exports rose 20.1% to $10.25 billion, driven by a 29.1% increase in mining shipments, including a 22.7% rise in copper exports. Silver exports surged 166.4%, while gold and lithium carbonate shipments jumped 73.3% and 134.5%, respectively. Exports of agricultural, forestry, and fishing products increased 15.6%, while industrial exports rose 6%. Meanwhile, imports edged up 0.2% to $8.26 billion, with consumer goods purchases rising 6.3% and intermediate goods increasing 0.2%, while capital goods imports fell 6.1%. Among consumer goods, imports of motor vehicles, cellphones, meat, and computers rose 49.7%, 32.6%, 34.4%, and 24.3%, respectively.

### 2026-08-07 21:16:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Used Car Prices MoM #Importance 1

US Used Car Prices Fall in July — The Manheim Used Vehicle Value Index, a widely followed measure of wholesale used-vehicle prices in the US market, decreased 1.4% month-over-month in July 2026, the first decline in three months, after edging up 0.1% in June. EV prices decreased 2.1% and non-EV were down 1.1%. Year-on-year, used-vehicle prices increased 1.3%, the smallest annual gain so far this year, led by rises in EVs (10.5%) and compact cars (4.1%). Prices also increased slightly for non-EVs (0.4%) and luxury (0.8%). In contrast, decreases were seen for pickups (-2.3%), SUVs (-1.7%) and midsize cars (-1.4%). “Wholesale values kept normalizing through July, continuing the pattern since the spring bounce peaked in March", according to Jonathan Gregory, Senior Director, Cox Automotive. "Gas prices reversed course and moved higher again as July went on, after briefly easing in June. That’s kept demand for used EVs firm even as the broader market cools", he added.

TSX Futures Rise as Rate Hike Bets Fade — Futures tracking the S&P/TSX Composite Index moved higher on Friday after the release of Canadian employment data and a key US jobs report. Canada's unemployment rate edged down 0.1 percentage point to 6.4%, slightly below forecasts of 6.5%, while employment increased by 75,000, well above expectations for a 15,000 gain. In the US, the economy unexpectedly shed 23,000 jobs, compared with forecasts for an 80,000 increase. The strong Canadian labor backdrop and softer US employment conditions limited risks of a hawkish Fed while strengthening the cast for a potential hike by the BoC, should energy prices remain higher. Meanwhile, oil prices were volatile as markets assessed Iran-Oman negotiations, easing energy inflation concerns and supporting financial stocks and the broader index. Gold prices also rose as expectations of US rate hikes receded, boosting mining stocks.

【Surprise US payrolls lift yen; traders stay wary of Japanese intervention risk】Surprise weakness in US nonfarm payrolls sent the yen sharply higher on Friday, but traders remained cautious about renewed Japanese intervention after Tokyo and Washington jointly stepped into FX markets only days earlier to support the weak currency. USD/JPY slid as much as 1.1% to about 156.68, well below July's 40-year peak of 163.99. It is unclear whether Japanese authorities participated in the move. Moves at the short end of the US yield curve reflected dollar weakness and a softer Fed tightening outlook. Mitsubishi UFJ senior FX strategist Lee Hardman said: "Nonfarm payrolls were much lower than expected, so dollar weakness is reasonable—look at the front end of the US yield curve. This is a rare downside surprise and clearly weakens market expectations for Fed tightening. We expect a marked market reaction and broad dollar selling."

【July global food price index highest since Jan 2023】The UN Food and Agriculture Organization said in a July 7 report its global food price index rose 0.6% MoM to 131.1 in July, the highest since January 2023 and 18.2% below the March 2022 peak. The cereals index climbed 3.4% MoM: wheat +5.8% on Black Sea export disruptions and heat in major producing areas; maize +3.6% amid US heat/drought and firmer energy prices; rice was broadly stable. The vegetable oils index gained 2.0% MoM to a high not seen since June 2022 as palm and soybean oil tracked higher crude; sugar rose 5.6% MoM on European heat and drought. Meat and dairy prices fell 2.8% and 0.7% MoM, respectively.

Treasury Yields Fall Sharply After Weak Jobs Report — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note fell sharply by 7 basis points to 4.6% on Friday after a weaker-than-expected jobs report scaled back bets on Fed rate hikes. Still, he would be prepared to raise rates in September if inflation readings released in the coming weeks come in hot, the FT reported.

【US July nonfarm payrolls unexpectedly fall; prior months revised down】US employers cut nonfarm payrolls by 23,000 in July and prior months were sharply revised down, the BLS said on Friday. The unemployment rate fell to 4.1% while the labor force participation rate continued to drift lower. The report flags a potential softening in the labor market amid rising inflation and heightened uncertainty from the Iran conflict, though still-robust consumer demand is sustaining some hiring. The print could encourage the Fed to delay further rate hikes as policymakers balance inflation pressures against employment risks.

【梁文鋒打新宇樹，王興興回應】8月7日下午，宇樹科技首次公開發行股票並在科創板上市網上投資者交流會召開。
據中國基金報訊息，宇樹科技董事長、總經理兼技術長王興興在會上首次回應稱，深度求索參與宇樹科技IPO戰略配售，是基於雙方簽訂的《戰略合作備忘錄》。
據宇樹7日釋出的最新公告，宇樹科技與深度求索將在三個方面合作：
一是面向通用人工智慧開展合作研發。宇樹科技與深度求索將結合業務發展的具體需求，在同等條件下選擇彼此作為合作伙伴，聯合開展人工智慧大模型與具身智慧相關技術的合作研發與產品開發，結成人工智慧領域的強強聯合。
二是在高效能通用機器人方面深度合作。未來，深度求索在開展具身智慧相關業務或應用探索時，將在同等條件下優先選擇與宇樹科技在高效能通用機器人採購及技術方案上開展業務合作。
三是在AI大模型方面開展深度合作。在具身智慧領域，宇樹科技將持續加大具身大模型相關研發投入與應用開發，未來在開展大模型相關適配呼叫及融合開發時，將在同等條件下優先選擇與深度求索在相關模型訓練服務及技術方案上開展業務合作。（長安街知事）

### 2026-08-07 20:38:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Employment Change #Importance 1

Canada Employment Rises More Than Expected — Employment in Canada increased by 75,100 in July 2026, well above market expectations for a 15,000 gain, following a revised 18,200 increase in June. The employment level rose 0.4% month-over-month, driven by a 57,900 increase in private-sector jobs after a 31,600 gain in the previous month, while public-sector employment fell by 27,000, extending June's 31,000 decline. By industry, employment increased in wholesale and retail trade, finance, insurance, real estate, rental and leasing (+18,000), professional, scientific and technical services (+17,000), and construction (+16,000). In contrast, jobs declined in public administration (-15,000) and agriculture (-9,600). Regionally, employment rose for a third consecutive month in Quebec (+52,000), alongside gains in British Columbia (+18,000), Manitoba (+5,900), and Nova Scotia (+4,600). Meanwhile, the employment rate edged up 0.1 percentage point to 60.9%.

### 2026-08-07 20:28:49  #其他

【工業和資訊化部新增配置P頻段資源助力應對極端天氣】記者8月7日獲悉，為應對當前極端天氣頻發、重發情況，工業和資訊化部日前為氣象部門新增配置P頻段無線電頻率資源，用於北斗衛星導航氣象探空系統技術試驗，提升我國應對極端天氣能力。據悉，2023年，工業和資訊化部為北斗衛星導航氣象探空系統規劃了P頻段無線電頻率資源，指導各地氣象部門做好北斗衛星導航氣象探空站和地面接收站的規範建設和臺站設定，用於短時降水預報、颱風監測、精細化智慧網格預報及天氣系統分析等場景，有效提高了高空觀測資料質量，助力我國高空觀測整體能力達到世界先進水平。此次新增配置P頻段無線電頻率資源，用於極端天氣下重放球（備份）、汛期加密觀測、平漂觀測等業務試驗，將進一步提升氣象部門防災減災、增強應對氣候變化特別是極端天氣能力。（新華社）

Siebert Financial CIO Mark Malek said recent moves have been driven by Iran-related headlines and government commentary, and that Trump’s remark likely supported the early rally. ### 2026-08-07 20:14:17  #Trading Economics #Economy #Chile #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Chile Inflation Hits Four-Month Low — Chile's annual inflation rate eased to 3.5% in July 2026 from 4.3% in June, marking the lowest annual CPI increase since March. The main upward pressures came from restaurants and lodging (6.6%), education (5.9%), healthcare (5.1%), transport (4.6%), recreation, sports, and culture (4.4%), and food and non-alcoholic beverages (4.0%). In contrast, prices fell for clothing and footwear (-4.8%) and insurance and financial services (-3.4%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.1% after remaining unchanged in June, bringing year-to-date inflation to 2.9%. Core consumer prices increased 0.4% month-on-month after declining 0.1% in June.

### 2026-08-07 20:06:19  #Trading Economics #Economy #Mexico #Auto Exports #Importance 1

Mexico Auto Exports Fall Again in July — Mexico's car exports fell 9.7% year-on-year to 261,534 units in July 2026, following a 9.2% decline in June. The decline reflected continued uncertainty in the auto sector as changing US trade policies clouded manufacturers' export plans. US President Trump in his second term imposed a series of tariffs on multiple countries, including Mexico. The Trump administration also decided not to renew the USMCA, the agreement will remain in force for another decade unless a member withdraws, but with annual reviews that could lead to significant changes. The US and Mexico also continue bilateral trade negotiations. Export volumes declined sharply for Mercedes (-97.7%), Nissan (-75.6%), Mazda (-35.4%), and Honda (-21.3%).

### 2026-08-07 20:03:40  #Trading Economics #Economy #Mexico #Car Production #Importance 1

Mexico Car Output Falls for 2nd Month — Mexico's car production fell 2.2% year-on-year to 302,673 units in July 2026, following a 1.9% decline in June. The result reflected continued uncertainty in the auto sector as changing US trade policies clouded manufacturers' production plans. US President Trump in his second term imposed a series of tariffs on multiple countries, including Mexico. While the Trump administration decided not to renew the USMCA, the agreement will remain in force for another decade unless a member withdraws, with annual reviews that could lead to significant changes. The US and Mexico also continue bilateral trade negotiations. Output declined sharply at Mercedes (-98%), Honda (-26%), Stellantis (-20.3%), and Nissan (-18.9%).

【摩根大通：Prosus當前折價“過高”，看好下半年估值修復】Prosus（PRX）股價週五一度上漲3.3%，表現優於大盤。摩根大通分析師Marcus Diebel在最新報告中指出，隨著下半年負面訊息週期開始緩解，Prosus當前的估值折價可能“過高”。受相關新聞影響，以及Just Eat和iFood的投資需求等因素影響，Prosus及其母公司Naspers今年的市場表現均不盡如人意，股價年內迄今已下跌約20%。目前，Prosus的交易價格較其淨資產價值（NAV）折讓約37%，而近期其合理的NAV折價區間約為20%左右。儘管面臨短期壓力，但分析師認為積極因素正在積聚。當前，圍繞外賣業務的負面新聞“似乎已經過去”，而Prosus在外賣以外的更廣泛投資組合繼續表現良好。摩根大通建議，Prosus和Naspers的管理層可以透過制定更細緻的部門財務目標來改善投資者的認知，從而推動股票重新估值，以縮小當前過高的折價空間。

【浙江省委書記王浩檢查督導排程各部門各系統防汛防颱工作】據浙江日報，7日，浙江省委書記王浩檢查督導排程各部門各系統防汛防颱工作。在省電力公司，王浩聽取電力防汛防颱情況彙報，強調要堅持底線思維、極限思維，總結運用好防禦颱風行之有效的做法，全力做好各項工作準備，切實做到隱患覆盤“改到位”、重點使用者“保到位”、應急前置“先到位”，確保遇有突發情況能第一時間處置、最快速度恢復。當前正處於迎峰度夏的關鍵時期，全省用電負荷連創歷史新高。要千方百計做好電力供給和執行排程工作，關鍵時刻拉得出、頂得上，全力保障居民、企業和社會用電需求。在影片連線省氣象局、省海洋經濟廳、浙江海事局、省水利廳時，王浩強調，要按照省委、省政府對防禦颱風“白海豚”的部署安排，狠抓工作落實、形成工作合力，把各項工作做紮實做細緻做到位。要堅持早報快報多報，特別是加強暴雨、小流域山洪、地質災害、城市內澇等專題預報，進一步提高預報及時性、精準度。要嚴格落實好各項海上管控措施，做到全員全域海上人員撤離到位，確保海上絕對安全。要抓住颱風到來前的“視窗期”，堅持早動快動小動，制定河湖庫調控預案，全面落實好病險山塘水庫除險加固應急措施，切實築牢安全防線。

【歐洲冬季能源風險升溫，面臨柴油與天然氣雙重壓力】高盛全球大宗商品研究聯席主管薩曼莎·達特表示，歐洲正面臨冬季前柴油與天然氣供應雙重風險。資料顯示，歐洲基準天然氣期貨TTF本週下跌7%，至約54歐元/兆瓦時，但達特仍維持其對第三季度剩餘時間達到60歐元的預測。歐洲天然氣庫存目前約為57.87%，較2009年至2025年的平均水平低約18個百分點。7月歐洲西北部液化天然氣進口量低於預期，導致月底庫存僅約43%，增加冬季補庫壓力。達特預計，若中東能源出口恢復緩慢，歐洲TTF天然氣價格今年12月可能升至100歐元/兆瓦時以上，比原先50歐元/兆瓦時的基準預測高出110%；若霍爾木茲海峽航運恢復順利，價格則可能回落至40歐元附近，比原先預測低20%。

### 2026-08-07 19:33:54  #MKT News #Brent #Impact mixed

【Europe faces dual diesel and gas supply risk ahead of winter】Goldman Sachs global commodities co-head Samantha Dart said Europe faces dual diesel and natural gas supply risks ahead of winter. Benchmark TTF gas futures fell about 7% this week to roughly €54/MWh; Dart maintains a forecast for TTF to reach €60/MWh for the remainder of Q3. European gas storage is about 57.87%, roughly 18 percentage points below the 2009–2025 average. Northwest Europe saw weaker-than-expected LNG inflows in July, leaving end‑July inventories near 43% and raising winter refill pressure. Dart warned that if Middle East energy exports recover slowly, TTF could exceed €100/MWh in December (around 110% above a €50/MWh baseline); if Strait of Hormuz shipping normalises, prices could fall toward €40/MWh (roughly 20% below prior forecasts).

SpaceX held yesterday's rebound even as $101 billion in its shares were unlocked from their post-IPO holds. 【土耳其沙特巴基斯坦籤聯合防務協議】更新：今天（8月7日），土耳其總統埃爾多安、巴基斯坦總理夏巴茲訪問沙烏地阿拉伯，並同沙特王儲兼首相穆罕默德・本・薩勒曼在麥加舉行聯合防務會議，簽署聯合防務協議——該協議旨在“增強針對任何侵略行為的集體威懾力”，規定對三國中任何一國的武裝攻擊將被視為對三國的攻擊。該協議還進一步規定要加強三國間各個領域的防務合作。據一名土耳其官員稱，這一聯合防務協議“純屬防禦性質”，僅承諾為防禦目的提供相互支援，“不針對任何特定行為體”，而且向其他區域國家開放，並不廢除或取代這些國家之間或與其他國家及組織之間現有的任何雙邊或多邊協議。 分析人士指出，伊朗戰事影響外溢背景下，一些國家發現美國“靠不住了”，因而尋求加強彼此間安全合作，嘗試建立地區集體安全機制。（CCTV國際時訊）

【BlackRock: U.S. euro sales to support yen raise long-bond risk】BlackRock said the U.S. sold euros to support the yen without notifying European policymakers, a move that increases geopolitical risk and further weakens the appeal of long-dated sovereign debt. James Turner, head of EMEA global fixed income at BlackRock, said the intervention is unlikely to directly affect European government bonds but signals a slight deterioration in cross-country cooperation. ### 2026-08-07 19:14:04  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China #SSE Composite #CSI300

【Shenzhen, Shanghai exchanges propose LOF delisting rules to curb high‑premium speculation】The Shenzhen and Shanghai stock exchanges have opened a consultation to tighten LOF arrangements, proposing classified triggers and procedures for terminating LOF listings. Under authorization of the Fund Law, the exchanges would supplement the Fund Listing Rules to specify delisting cases, procedures, risk‑disclosure requirements and institutional responsibilities, aimed at addressing high‑premium on‑exchange speculation in LOFs. Market estimates indicate roughly 125 LOFs could be affected, with combined on‑exchange AUM around 26 bln yuan; about 91 are small‑scale LOFs with combined on‑exchange size near 300 mln yuan, so the overall number and scale appear limited. Exchanges note delisting does not equate to fund liquidation, will not alter normal investment operations, and does not affect off‑exchange holders’ subscription/redemption rights. On‑exchange holders can transfer to off‑exchange custody during a transition or redeem/sell on‑exchange; after delisting holders can still redeem via on‑ or off‑exchange distribution channels. The exchanges say delisting will not involve funds net‑selling underlying equities and should have limited direct impact on underlying markets.

【自然資源部啟動浙江海洋災害三級應急響應】自然資源部8月7日下午召開臺風“白海豚”海洋和地質災害防禦排程會議，傳達學習國家防總專題會商會議精神，聽取部東海局、浙江省自然資源廳、福建省自然資源廳等單位防禦部署情況彙報，通報此次颱風過程可能引發海洋和地質災害情況，全面部署災害防範應對工作。會議強調要全力做好海洋災害預警報和防禦工作，一要提高政治站位，深刻認識海洋災害防禦工作形勢；二要加強會商研判，提前預報預警；三要加強地方指導，注重聯防聯控；四要加強海上船舶，確保作業生產安全；五要加強科普宣傳，提升避險救災能力。會議要求全力做好強降雨引發地質災害風險防範應對，一是堅持下沉一線和靠前指揮，二是強化駐縣包鄉和技術支撐，三是強化趨勢研判和預報預警，四是強化臨災預警和轉移避險，五是加強值班值守和資訊報送。針對此次颱風過程，自然資源部已啟動浙江海洋災害三級應急響應，江蘇、上海、福建海洋災害四級應急響應，浙江、福建地質災害防禦IV級響應，派出專家組指導地方開展巡查排查、監測預警、會商研判，災情調查等災害防範應對工作。 (央視新聞)

【水利部針對6省市啟動洪水防禦Ⅳ級應急響應】據預報，受第13號颱風“白海豚”影響，8月8日至12日，浙江、福建、江西、江蘇、上海、安徽等地將有一次強降雨過程，以大到暴雨為主，區域性有大暴雨，並伴有短時強降雨；太湖及浙閩地區錢塘江、甬江、椒江、甌江、閩江，長江流域下游沿江支流滁河、青弋江、水陽江、巢湖和中游鄱陽湖水系、洞庭湖水系湘江，淮河干流及中游支流史灌河和淠河等河流將出現明顯漲水過程，其中部分主要河流可能發生編號洪水；暴雨區內部分中小河流可能發生較大洪水。依據《水利部水旱災害防禦應急響應工作規程》，水利部於8月7日18時針對上海、江蘇、浙江、安徽、福建、江西啟動洪水防禦Ⅳ級應急響應，督促指導地方水利部門密切關注颱風“白海豚”動向和雨水情變化，強化應急值守和會商研判，加強監測預報預警，科學排程水工程，做好江河防汛、水庫安全度汛、中小河流洪水和山洪災害防禦等工作，確保人民群眾生命財產安全。（新華社）

### 2026-08-07 19:08:02  #其他

【公安部發布《公安機關網路空間安全監督檢查辦法》】公安部8月7日釋出《公安機關網路空間安全監督檢查辦法》，自2026年10月1日起施行。 辦法共23條，主要規定六方面內容：一是明確監督檢查物件，包括網路運營者、資料處理者、個人資訊處理者等；二是明確監督檢查方式，包括線上巡查、線下核查，日常檢查、專項檢查等；三是明確監督檢查內容，包括被檢查物件是否履行法定的網路空間安全義務，以及重大活動安保期間的重點檢查內容等；四是明確監督檢查機制，要求在國家網路安全、資料安全等機制統籌協調下，加強部門協調、聯合檢查，減少企業負擔；五是明確檢查結果運用，公安機關按檢查情況採取督促整改、傳送公安提示函以及處罰等措施；六是明確相關法律責任，明確了公安機關及工作人員翫忽職守、濫用職權、洩露國家秘密等法律責任。 辦法對健全聯合檢查機制、減少企業負擔作出規定。公安機關應當建立健全與有關部門的監督檢查協作配合機制，加強跨部門協同配合、資訊溝通，合理確定現場檢查的頻次、範圍、方式，避免重複檢查、交叉檢查。 此外，辦法還對公安機關執法規範化建設提出進一步要求，明確公安機關開展監督檢查的人員、文書等具體要求，以及委託技術支援機構協助開展檢查的安全管理要求。(新華社)

【ChinaAMC: Short-term A-share tech volatility doesn't alter long-term AI thesis; bonds retain core allocation value】ChinaAMC says recent A-share tech swings reflect funding-side noise rather than a fundamental industry reversal; AI’s very-long-cycle growth thesis remains intact and sector opportunities are ample. Du Houliang notes some AI supply-chain names now trade at relatively low valuations, reflecting market pessimism, but low valuations alone are not sufficient to prompt a rebound. China’s bond-market pricing has moved back into a reasonable/neutral range; with policy still accommodative and bank funding costs heading lower, onshore bonds retain value as a steady core allocation. Wang Shen expects the low-rate environment to persist this year; with further declines in banks’ liability costs in H2, pure bonds should continue to provide steady core returns, though absolute returns may ease versus 1H.

### 2026-08-07 18:53:40  #Trading Economics #Economy #Portugal #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Portugal Trade Gap Widens in June — Portugal’s trade deficit widened to €3.63 billion in June 2026, up €1.12 billion from a year earlier, as imports grew significantly faster than exports. Imports surged 19.6% year-on-year, driven mainly by a 50.7% increase in fuels and lubricants, particularly crude oil from Brazil, reflecting higher prices. Industrial supplies also contributed strongly, rising 23.5%. Imports from Brazil, the Netherlands and Germany recorded particularly sharp increases. Exports, meanwhile, rose 10% year-on-year, supported by a 69.3% jump in fuels and lubricants and a 13.4% increase in industrial supplies, particularly base metals. Exports to Spain (13.2%) and Germany (6.2%) strengthened. In the second quarter, exports increased 10.3% year-on-year, while imports rose 8.7%. During the first half of 2026, exports were up 1.7% from a year earlier, compared with a 6.3% increase in imports.

### 2026-08-07 18:46:03  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China

【Hong Kong insurance lawmaker says tax rumour on offshore policy returns linked to CRS; no formal policy seen】Hong Kong Legislative Council insurance-sector lawmaker Chen Peiliang said rumours that mainland China will impose a 20% personal income tax on offshore insurance policy returns are tied to implementation of the Common Reporting Standard (CRS) and individual overseas income reporting arrangements; he said no formal policy documents or implementation details have been issued. Chen noted Chinese residents have long been required to declare and pay tax on overseas investment income and that the requirement covers all overseas investment returns rather than targeting insurance products. The topic flared after some mainland China regions began implementing CRS-related measures. He cited Hong Kong savings-style insurance yields of about 6–6.5% versus roughly 3% for comparable mainland products, saying Hong Kong policies would retain close to a two percentage-point net yield advantage even after tax and are therefore unlikely to see a sharp drop in mainland buyers. He added Hong Kong products remain competitive for multi-currency allocation and wealth transfer and that he does not plan to raise the matter in LegCo.

Sensex Finishes on Negative Note — India's BSE Sensex closed about 0.6% down at 78,499.2 on Friday, reversing gains from the previous two sessions, pressured by higher oil prices and heavyweights financials. Bajaj Finance slipped 5.9% to lead losses among stocks, after a draft RBI proposal raised concerns over restrictions on certain revolving-credit products offered by them. Bajaj Finserv, ICICI Bank, Trent, Bharti Airtel and Eternal were also among the major laggards.On the upside, technology stocks rallied, led by Tata Consultancy Services, Tech Mahindra, HCLTech and Infosys. State Bank of India gained around 1% after reporting a stronger-than-expected quarterly profit, supported by healthy loan growth.

【香港保險界議員陳沛良：境外保單徵稅傳聞與CRS執行有關，暫未見正式檔案】針對近日”內地對境外保單收益徵收20%個人所得稅”的傳聞，香港立法會保險界議員陳沛良回覆第一財經查詢時表示，有關討論主要與稅務”共同申報準則”（CRS）及個人境外收入申報安排有關，截至目前尚未收到有關部門釋出的正式政策檔案或執行細節，相關資訊均來自新聞報道。陳沛良指出，中國居民就境外投資收益依法申報及繳稅的要求一直存在，覆蓋所有境外投資收益，並非專門針對保險產品。近期由於內地部分割槽域開始實際執行相關準則，才引發市場熱議。在他看來，即使未來稅務安排有所調整，短期或對香港保險業構成影響，長遠對業界未必是壞事。CRS深化落實疊加資金跨境管理透明化，未來或催生更規範的閉環式資金渠道——資金出境投保，到期後有序迴流。他同時提到香港保單的收益空間：目前香港儲蓄型保險收益水平約6%至6.5%，內地同類產品約3%，即使扣除稅付，香港保單仍有接近兩個百分點的淨收益優勢，相信不會大幅削減內地訪客來港投保的意願。他認為香港保險產品設計靈活、可多元貨幣配置，在財富傳承與規劃上仍具競爭力。陳沛良還表示，暫無需在立法會就此提出質詢。（第一財經）

Brent Hovers Below $82 — Brent crude oil traded below $82 per barrel on Friday, moving between gains and losses as investors weighed negotiations between Iran and Oman over the Strait of Hormuz against renewed geopolitical tensions. Optimism over a partial reopening of the strategic waterway faded as Iran reportedly sought to exclude US and Israeli vessels and impose fees on countries it considers hostile. The US, meanwhile, has pushed for unrestricted transit and a return to pre-war conditions, leaving the two sides seemingly far apart on the terms of any agreement. President Donald Trump maintained an optimistic tone, saying the conflict could end “pretty soon” and that discussions were progressing. However, reports of Iran attacking “hostile targets” in the strait following explosions near Qeshm Island added to concerns over shipping security. Separately, Iran-backed Houthi militants said they had carried out a “large-scale” attack against forces aligned with Yemen’s Saudi-backed government.

【焦炭第三輪降價全面落地 短期內仍偏弱執行】焦炭第三輪降價全面落地，降幅溼熄焦50元/噸、幹熄焦55元/噸。第三輪降價落地的核心驅動力仍在於“負反饋”邏輯的深化，終端鋼材需求疲弱導致鋼廠利潤承壓，因此高爐檢修增多，鋼廠鐵水產量持續回落，下游對於原料採購邊際走弱。然近期期貨市場受成本端擾動和限產預期提振出現反彈；現貨市場在鋼廠虧損壓力下開啟第三輪提降，市場整體處於下行通道中，但降幅有望逐步收窄。綜合預計焦炭價格短期內仍偏弱執行，後期仍需重點關注鐵水復產情況以及焦化廠開工情況。 (我的鋼鐵網)

Greece Inflation Rate at 5-Month Low — The annual inflation rate in Greece fell to 3.4% in July 2026, a five-month low, from 4.4% in June. Prices slowed for housing & utilities (8.8% vs 10.6%), food and non-alcoholic beverages (0.4% vs 2.7%), restaurants & hotels (5.8% vs 7.7%), recreation & culture (1.3% vs 1.7%), and furnishings & household equipment (0.4% vs 0.5%). Also, inflation was steady for health (at 1.1%) and education (at 2.8%). On the other hand, cost accelerated for transport (7.4% vs 7.2%) and clothing & footwear (3.3% vs 0.5%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices fell 1.4%, after being flat in June.

### 2026-08-07 18:16:18  #Trading Economics #Economy #Mauritius #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Mauritius Inflation Rate at 7-Month High — The annual inflation rate in Mauritius accelerated to 4.4% in July 2026 from 3.7% in the previous month, reaching its highest level since December 2025. Prices rebounded for food and non-alcoholic beverages (2.7% vs. -1.3% in June), while inflation picked up for housing and utilities (8.9% vs. 8.8%), alcoholic beverages and tobacco (5.7% vs. 1.8%), furnishings, household equipment and routine household maintenance (4.3% vs. 3.5%), clothing and footwear (2.9% vs. 2.7%), and recreation, sports, and culture (2.5% vs. 2.3%). Meanwhile, inflation eased for health (4.7% vs. 6.7%), transport (4.6% vs. 9.5%), and restaurants and hotels (9.9% vs. 10.2%). At the same time, inflation was unchanged for education at 5.4%, while deflation for information and communication remained steady at 1.5%. On a monthly basis, consumer prices edged up 0.2% in July, easing from a 0.3% increase in June and marking the slowest monthly rise since March.

### 2026-08-07 18:15:28  #Trading Economics #Economy #Macedonia #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

North Macedonia Inflation Eases to 2-Year Low — The annual inflation rate in North Macedonia eased to 2.3% in July 2026 from 3.4% in the previous month, marking the lowest reading since August 2024. Price growth moderated for food and non-alcoholic beverages (1.1% vs 2.5% in June), clothing and footwear (1.8% vs 2.1%), furniture, household equipment, and routine household maintenance (2.1% vs 4.2%), health (1.3% vs 1.8%), transportation (1.1% vs 6.2%), information and communication (2.0% vs 2.8%), restaurants and hotels (4.4% vs 4.9%), and insurance and financial services (1.6% vs 1.7%). In contrast, costs increased for alcoholic beverages and tobacco (9.5% vs 9.3%), recreation and culture (5.7% vs 5.1%), education (4.9% vs 4.6%), housing and utilities (3.6% vs 2.8%), and miscellaneous goods and services (1.9% vs 1.6%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices grew by 0.1% in July, rebounding from a 0.5% drop in the preceding period.

### 2026-08-07 18:12:18  #其他

【工信部新增配置P頻段資源 助力氣象部門提升應對極端天氣能力】為應對當前汛期強對流、暴雨、颱風等極端災害性天氣頻發、重發、併發情況，工業和資訊化部近日為氣象部門新增配置P頻段無線電頻率資源用於北斗衛星導航氣象探空系統技術試驗，提升我國應對極端天氣能力。2023年，工業和資訊化部為北斗衛星導航氣象探空系統規劃了P頻段無線電頻率資源，指導各地氣象部門做好北斗衛星導航氣象探空站和地面接收站的規範建設和臺站設定，用於短時降水預報、颱風監測、精細化智慧網格預報及天氣系統分析等場景，有效提高了高空觀測資料質量，助力我國高空觀測整體能力達到世界先進水平。此次新增配置P頻段無線電頻率資源，用於極端天氣下重放球（備份）、汛期加密觀測、平漂觀測等業務試驗，將進一步提升氣象部門防災減災、增強應對氣候變化特別是極端天氣能力。下一步，工業和資訊化部將立足天氣預報預測和氣象防災減災現實需要，持續為我國氣象事業高質量發展提供可靠頻率資源服務保障。

### 2026-08-07 18:01:21  #其他

【網安監督檢查新規釋出 明確檢查物件、內容等】8月7日，公安部發布《公安機關網路空間安全監督檢查辦法》，自2026年10月1日起施行。《公安機關網路空間安全監督檢查辦法》共23條，主要規定六方面內容：一是明確監督檢查物件，包括網路運營者、資料處理者、個人資訊處理者等；二是明確監督檢查方式，包括線上巡查、線下核查，日常檢查、專項檢查等；三是明確監督檢查內容，包括被檢查物件是否履行法定的網路空間安全義務，以及重大活動安保期間的重點檢查內容等；四是明確監督檢查機制，要求在國家網路安全、資料安全等機制統籌協調下，加強部門協調、聯合檢查，減少企業負擔；五是明確檢查結果運用，公安機關按檢查情況採取督促整改、傳送公安提示函以及處罰等措施；六是明確相關法律責任，明確了公安機關及工作人員翫忽職守、濫用職權、洩露國家秘密等法律責任。（央視新聞）

【主流房貸利率仍未跌破3% “2”字頭僅是少數銀行在部分地區行為】近期，一些城市傳出房貸利率跌破3%、進入“2字頭”的訊息。今日從銀行、房產中介等多方瞭解到，低於3%的房貸利率只是少數外資銀行在部分地區推出的專屬優惠，當前全國主流房貸利率仍穩定在3.0%至3.1%區間。招聯首席經濟學家董希淼分析稱，這一低利率主要面向特定合作渠道且需透過嚴苛資質審批，多數普通購房者能申請到的仍是3%左右的主流市場利率。因此，2.7%左右的房貸利率是競爭“特例”，體現外資銀行的靈活經營優勢，而非房貸利率普遍下行訊號。（中新經緯）

【Trump approval weakens as domestic, foreign pressures mount】A Quinnipiac University poll last week put President Trump’s approval at a record-low 32% and found 68% of voters say he is not sufficiently focused on ordinary Americans’ problems. Supporters and GOP insiders say his standing is being undermined by an unpopular war, tariffs and high-profile White House renovation and memorial projects that are consuming his time. Republicans report donor fatigue after Trump has declined to deploy roughly $400m held by his MAGA super PAC, and some party figures warn his priorities could cost the GOP the House and possibly the Senate in the midterms. Several Republicans are urging a shift to cost-of-living and economic messaging; Trump has shown no sign of changing course. Separately, Trump’s push for a national ID requirement in the Save America Act is viewed by Republicans as unlikely to clear the Senate.

### 2026-08-07 17:46:03  #Trading Economics #Economy #Taiwan #Imports YoY #Importance 0

Taiwan Import Growth Misses Forecasts — Imports to Taiwan climbed 37.4% year-on-year to USD 58.1 billion in July 2026, slowing from a 51.8% surge in June and falling short of market expectations for a 48.4% increase. Growth was driven by purchases of machinery and equipment, which rose 55.8% and accounted for 66.7% of total imports, led by electronic parts (61.5%) and information, communication and audio products (18.8%). Imports of mineral products, which represented 9.8% of the total, increased 25.9%. Mainland China and Hong Kong remained the largest source of imports, accounting for 19.9% of the total, followed by South Korea at 13.6%. In the first seven months of 2026, total imports reached USD 377.3 billion, up 39.8% from the same period a year earlier.

【中證協：券商出具債券投資建議時 原則上單一債券投顧賬戶持倉規模不超過25%】中證協制定釋出《證券公司債券投資顧問業務管理規則》，旨在強化券商債券投顧業務自律管理，規範機構展業及服務模式。新規重點明確債券投顧業務持倉集中度風控要求，劃定硬性監管紅線：券商出具債券投資建議時，原則上不得導致單一債券投顧賬戶單券持倉成本，超過該賬戶總資產規模的25%，嚴控單一債券持倉集中風險。同時新規明確三類豁免情形，具體包括投資標的為國債、央行票據、政策性金融債、地方政府債、境內系統重要性銀行同業存單；投顧首次服務對應賬戶的三個月內；因債券回售、系統重要性銀行名單調整等客觀因素導致的被動超限情況。

### 2026-08-07 17:37:29  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China

【China Securities Association: firms may not use client excess returns as bond adviser performance metric】The China Securities Association issued Management Rules for Securities Firms’ Bond Investment Advisory Business, setting a six-month transition and taking effect Feb. 5, 2027. Firms must establish scientific, reasonable compensation and performance-evaluation systems that emphasize compliance in business conduct and client satisfaction. Firms must include cases where bond advisers fail to exercise due diligence or prudence — causing material risk or client complaints — in staff assessments. The rules prohibit using client excess returns directly as a performance metric for bond investment-advisory personnel.

### 2026-08-07 17:36:04  #Trading Economics #Economy #Taiwan #Exports YoY #Importance 0

Taiwan Exports Rise Less Than Expected — Exports from Taiwan surged 32.9% year-on-year to USD 75.3 billion in July 2026, easing from a 40.3% jump in June and missing market expectations of a 40.7% increase. Growth was driven by strong demand for electronic products, with shipments rising 50.5%, while exports of information and communication products increased 29.5%. The US remained the largest export destination, accounting for 31% of total shipments, with exports rising 25.2%, followed by Mainland China and Hong Kong, where shipments increased 33.4%. Exports continued to benefit from robust demand for AI and high-performance computing applications, supporting shipments across Taiwan's semiconductor and AI hardware supply chains. The Ministry of Finance expects exports to grow 31%-35% year-on-year in August. In the first seven months of 2026, total exports reached USD 493.0 billion, up 44.7% from the same period a year earlier.

【黑龍江、青海“風光打捆”綠電首次“組團”進京】記者7日從首都電力交易中心獲悉，首都電力交易中心聯合黑龍江、青海電力交易中心，於2026年夏季首次開展黑龍江、青海“風光打捆”綠電進京交易。 本次“風光打捆”綠電交易覆蓋度夏及秋冬季用電視窗期，計劃輸送綠電2.34億千瓦時（青海1.03億千瓦時、黑龍江1.31億千瓦時）。其中，夏季用電高峰期間，黑龍江、青海“組團”輸送綠電將達4700萬千瓦時。 “青海光伏白天出力較多，黑龍江風電夜間出力較多，根據不同的發電特性進行最佳化配置，開展晝夜互補、接力交易。”首都電力交易中心副總經理王立介紹。 今年以來，北京不斷擴大外調綠電供給，透過創新交易組織模式、拓展綠電應用場景、服務客戶多元需求，持續擴容北京綠電“朋友圈”。目前，已有來自華北、東北、西北、西南、華中五大區域的16個省（自治區）及地區向北京輸送綠電，今年上半年綠電交易規模超200億千瓦時，跨區綠電交易電量同比增長88%，有效擴充北京綠電供給與消費體量。(新華社)

Greece Trade Deficit Narrows in June — Greece’s trade deficit narrowed to EUR 2.6 billion in June 2026 from EUR 3.2 billion in the same month a year earlier, although it remained the largest deficit recorded in the past three months. Exports surged 26.3% year-on-year to EUR 5.0 billion, supported by stronger shipments to both EU countries (29.8%) and third countries (21.4%). Meanwhile, imports rose 6.5% to EUR 7.6 billion, driven by higher purchases from both EU countries (4.8%) and third countries (8.6%). Over the January–June period, the trade deficit narrowed to EUR 15.1 billion from EUR 16.9 billion in the corresponding period of the previous year.

【日本四大壽險公司債券賬面浮虧達960億美元 日本金融廳予以密切關注】日本四大壽險公司公佈的資料顯示，截至6月底的三個月內，它們持有的日本國內債券未實現虧損合計增加7%，凸顯出利率大幅上升給壽險行業帶來的風險。根據日本生命保險等四家公司公佈的4月至6月季度業績報告，這些保險公司所持債券的賬面浮虧擴大至15.13萬億日元（約960億美元）。除日本生命保險外，另外三家公司分別是第一生命控股、住友生命保險和明治安田生命保險。四家公司的債券未實現虧損均有所增加。壽險公司通常會持有日本國債及其他債務證券直至到期，以匹配其保險負債，因此這些賬面虧損一般不會立即轉化為實際虧損。不過，如果大量客戶集中退保，保險公司可能需要出售這些債券來籌集資金支付退保款項。屆時，賬面浮虧可能轉化為實際損失，從而對保險公司的盈利和投資活動造成壓力。

### 2026-08-07 17:14:37  #Trading Economics #Economy #Malta #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Malta Industrial Activity Shrinks in June — Industrial production in Malta fell 1.5% year-on-year in June 2026, reversing an upwardly revised 1.0% increase in May. Manufacturing output declined 1.4%, following a 1.4% gain in the previous month, as lower production of food products (-0.6%) and chemical and pharmaceutical products (-1.4%) outweighed a sharp increase in wood, paper products and printing (9.0%). By industrial grouping, output fell further for consumer goods (-7.6% vs -3.8%) and non-durable consumer goods (-6.8% vs -5.9%), while durable consumer goods production swung to a 15.0% contraction from a 15.1% increase. Meanwhile, declines eased for capital goods (-2.9% vs -7.4%) and energy (-3.5% vs -6.8%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial production decreased 0.6%, following a downwardly revised 5.0% drop in May.

### 2026-08-07 17:14:13  #Trading Economics #Economy #Croatia #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Croatia Trade Deficit Narrows in June — Croatia’s trade deficit narrowed to EUR 1.71 billion in June 2026 from EUR 1.81 billion a year earlier. Exports rose by 7.7% year-on-year to EUR 2.26 billion, while imports increased by 1.9% to EUR 3.98 billion. In the first half of 2026, the trade gap decreased to EUR 10.31 billion from EUR 10.42 billion in the corresponding period of the previous year. During this period, exports grew by 10.8% to EUR 13.70 billion, lifted by stronger shipments to both EU countries (14.1%) and non-EU markets (3.9%). Meanwhile, imports increased by 4.8% to EUR 23.89 billion, driven largely by higher purchases from both the EU (3.8%) and non-EU markets (8.4%).

### 2026-08-07 17:10:51  #Trading Economics #Markets #Euro Area #Currency #Importance 0

Euro Eases from 7-Week High — The euro traded around $1.152 on Friday, slightly below Wednesday’s seven-week high of $1.155, as investors assessed the outlook for energy prices and monetary policy. Oil prices remained lower for the week but extended their gains over the past two sessions, with renewed supply concerns raising fears that higher energy costs could keep inflation elevated. Iran and Oman are reportedly working on an agreement to establish transit routes through the Strait of Hormuz, although no deal has yet been announced. Concerns intensified after Iran published a restrictive draft plan that could limit access for US and Israeli vessels and require compensation from countries it considers hostile. Still, investors remain hopeful that a deal to reopen the waterway would eventually lower oil prices and ease inflationary pressures. Meanwhile, the ECB left rates unchanged in July following a 25-basis-point hike in June, its first increase in three years, amid renewed energy-driven inflation pressures.

Sterling Edges Down — The British pound edged down to around $1.344 on Friday as investors remained cautious over the outcome of Iran-Oman talks and whether they will lead to the restoration of energy flows through the Strait of Hormuz. Concerns over potential supply disruptions also persisted after Iran published a restrictive draft plan that could limit access for US and Israeli vessels and require compensation from countries it considers hostile. Investors continued to assess the implications for the energy outlook and monetary policy, with oil prices rising over the past two sessions despite remaining lower for the week. ### 2026-08-07 17:01:17  #Trading Economics #Economy #Singapore #Foreign Exchange Reserves #Importance 1

Singapore Forex Reserves Retreat in July — Singapore's official foreign exchange reserves declined to SGD 549.3 billion in July 2026 from SGD 551.3 billion in the previous month, easing from the highest level since February 2022. The decline was mainly driven by lower gold and foreign exchange holdings, which fell to SGD 539.2 billion from SGD 541.2 billion in June. Special drawing rights also edged down to SGD 8.25 billion from SGD 8.29 billion. Meanwhile, Singapore's reserve position in the IMF increased slightly to SGD 1.87 billion from SGD 1.82 billion. In the same month a year earlier, official foreign exchange reserves stood at SGD 515.5 billion.

UK 10-Year Gilt Yield Hovers Above 3-Week Low — The UK 10-year gilt yield held near 4.95% on Friday, remaining above Tuesday’s three-week low of 4.891%, as higher oil prices revived concerns that central banks may need to keep interest rates elevated. Brent crude extended its gains for a second session amid supply concerns after Iran published a restrictive draft plan for the Strait of Hormuz. Although Iran and Oman are reportedly working on an agreement to establish transit routes through the waterway, no deal has yet been announced, keeping investors cautious. Despite the recent rise in yields, the UK 10-year yield remains around 9 basis points lower this week, supported by hopes that a reopening of the strait could eventually ease oil prices and inflation expectations. Meanwhile, the Bank of England left interest rates unchanged in July, with Governor Andrew Bailey saying the disinflation process remained on track despite ongoing external risks.

【今日收官 五大上市險企超千億元分紅創歷史新高】隨著新華保險2025年度末期現金紅利正式派發，A股五大上市險企年度分紅派息全面收官。中國人保、中國人壽、中國平安、中國太保、新華保險2025年全年累計現金分紅總額首次突破千億元，達到1023.94億元，創歷史新高；較2024年的907.89億元增加116.05億元，同比增幅為12.78%。至此，五家上市險企分紅頻次將從“一年一次”全面邁向“一年兩次”。上市險企集中推進中期分紅、持續加大分紅力度，是看好行業基本面、看好資本市場長期走勢的直接體現。五家上市險企一致認為，中國經濟長期向好的基本面沒有改變，產業升級與科技創新紅利正持續釋放，為資本市場提供了堅實支撐。多家機構明確表示，將持續加大權益配置規模，進一步發揮保險資金作為長期資本、耐心資本的“壓艙石”作用，以實際行動支援資本市場平穩健康發展，與投資者共享中國經濟與資本市場長期發展的成果。（金融時報）

【Five listed insurers' 2025 cash dividends hit CNY102.39bn, record high; payouts shift to twice-yearly】With New China Life's 2025 year-end cash dividend paid, five listed insurers — PICC, China Life, Ping An, CPIC and New China Life — completed 2025 dividend distributions, with cumulative cash payouts reaching CNY102.39 billion, the first time above CNY100 billion. That compares with CNY90.79 billion in 2024, an increase of CNY11.61 billion (+12.78% YoY). The insurers are accelerating mid-year (interim) dividends and moving dividend frequency from once-yearly to twice-yearly, increasing dividend intensity. They cited an unchanged long-term positive outlook for China’s economy and ongoing gains from industrial upgrading and tech innovation as support for capital markets. Several institutional investors said they will increase equity allocations and deploy insurance funds as long-term, patient capital to support market stability.

### 2026-08-07 16:49:47  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Germany 10-Year Bund Yield Rises for 2nd Session — Germany’s 10-year Bund yield rose to 3.148% on Friday, extending gains for a second session and moving further from Wednesday’s three-week low of 3.10%. The rise in yields came as higher oil prices revived concerns that persistent inflationary pressures could limit the scope for central banks to ease monetary policy. Investors remained cautious amid uncertainty over a lasting agreement to reopen the Strait of Hormuz, while Brent crude extended its gains for a second session following reports that Iran could restrict US and Israeli vessels from using the waterway and seek compensation from countries it considers hostile. The European Central Bank left interest rates unchanged at its July meeting after delivering a 25-basis-point hike in June, its first increase in three years, amid renewed energy-driven inflation pressures. Meanwhile, German industrial production rose more than expected in June, adding to signs that Europe’s largest economy could finally be gaining momentum.

Hong Kong Stocks End Higher After Early Losses — The Hang Seng Index edged up 0.5%, or 138 points, to close at 25,668 on Friday, recovering from an early decline as late-session buying helped the benchmark index finish in positive territory. The market came under pressure in the morning as investors locked in recent gains, but sentiment improved later in the day amid renewed interest in technology and selected heavyweight stocks. The rebound was supported by optimism over China’s economic outlook following stronger-than-expected trade data, which boosted confidence in the resilience of the country’s export sector. Beyond equities, China is also pushing to strengthen Hong Kong’s position as an international gold trading and storage center. Despite the modest advance, trading remained cautious as investors weighed global market conditions, interest-rate expectations, and the pace of China’s economic recovery. Notable movers included MiniMax (9.8%), Z.AI Co. (14.6%), Kingboard Laminates (9.9%), SMIC (2.4%), and Xiaomi (0.7%).

### 2026-08-07 16:45:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #Bulgaria #Consumer Confidence #Importance 1

Bulgaria Consumer Confidence Improves in Q3 — Bulgaria’s consumer confidence index rose to -25.7 in the third quarter of 2026 from -28.6 in the previous quarter. Consumers became less pessimistic regarding the 12-month outlook for the general economy (-25.4 vs -33.1 in Q2) and their financial situation (-23 vs -25.6). At the same time, inflation expectations eased significantly (14.4 vs 26.4), suggesting that households expect a slower pace of price increases over the coming year. Moreover, consumers also had a better assessment of their ability to purchase durable goods, both in the current period (-40.7 vs -44.4) and over the next 12 months (-15.8 vs -17). Expectations for household savings also improved (-56.4 vs -60.8). Meanwhile, concerns about the labour market increased, with unemployment expectations worsening (19.2 vs 18.2).

【廣西壯族自治區北海市人大常委會原副主任蔣同根被“雙開”】中央紀委國家監委網站8月7日通報，據廣西壯族自治區紀委監委訊息：經廣西壯族自治區黨委批准，廣西壯族自治區紀委監委對北海市人大常委會原黨組成員、副主任蔣同根嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。經查，蔣同根喪失理想信念，背棄初心使命，對黨不忠誠不老實，對抗組織審查；違背組織原則，隱瞞不報個人有關事項；廉潔底線失守，違規收受禮品禮金和消費卡，接受可能影響公正執行公務的宴請；私慾膨脹，將公權力異化為謀取私利的工具，大搞權錢交易，利用職務便利在專案承攬、專案推進等方面為他人謀利，並非法收受鉅額財物。蔣同根嚴重違反黨的政治紀律、組織紀律和廉潔紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經自治區紀委常委會會議研究並報自治區黨委批准，決定給予蔣同根開除黨籍處分；由自治區監委給予其開除公職處分；終止其北海市第十二次黨代會代表資格；收繳其違紀違法所得；將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。

【全球食品價格升至三年來的最高水平】由於對關鍵糧食出口通道的擔憂加劇，加之全球主要產區遭遇惡劣天氣，全球食品價格7月小幅上漲，達到三年多來的最高水平。聯合國糧農組織週五釋出的報告顯示，7月份聯合國食品商品價格指數環比上漲0.6%，其中糧食、糖和植物油價格漲幅居前。全球糧食市場的關注點已從美伊戰爭初期引發的動盪，轉向收成疲軟、貿易不確定性和持續的天氣擔憂。俄烏之間升級的襲擊行動，加劇了市場對黑海這一關鍵糧食出口地區供應的擔憂，推動小麥價格在6月份升至兩年來的最高水平。與此同時，極端高溫導致作物受損，歐洲正面臨有記錄以來最嚴重的糧食減產之一，而美國主要產區的乾旱也威脅著玉米和大豆的產量。伊朗衝突導致化肥供應中斷和能源成本上漲，再加上厄爾尼諾現象異常強勁 ，意味著全球食品市場正面臨新一輪的生產和貿易風險。

Market focus has shifted to weak harvests, trade uncertainty and extreme weather. Escalating attacks in the Russia‑Ukraine war have intensified supply concerns for Black Sea exports, lifting wheat to a two‑year high in June. Iran‑related fighting has disrupted fertilizer supplies and pushed energy costs higher, and a strong El Nifio raises further production and trade risks for global food markets.

### 2026-08-07 16:28:22  #MKT News #Important #Commodities #Industrial Metals #Market Regions #Greater China

【Eight polysilicon producers pledge not to sell below full cost; may add penalties】Eight domestic polysilicon producers signed a voluntary pledge to stop low‑price dumping, not sell below full cost, and to carry out self‑inspection under regulator oversight. The China Nonferrous Metals Industry Association (Silicon Branch) says the domestic polysilicon market is in a wait‑and‑see standoff with trading stalled; last week average transaction prices for N‑type feedstock and granular silicon fell to 32,000 yuan/ton and 31,000 yuan/ton respectively, markedly below industry full‑cost estimates. Industry sources say prices would need to rise more than 40% to reach expected full‑cost levels. A signatory said firms are acting on recent MIIT and SAMR meetings, the pledge is voluntary, and punitive measures to strengthen self‑discipline may be introduced after the announcement.

【八家矽料企業釋出倡議 知情人士稱或配套懲罰措施】八家光伏矽料企業簽署《倡議書》，承諾不低於完全成本價銷售，杜絕低價傾銷，並將自查自糾、接受監管。記者注意到，當前國內矽料成交價格已顯著低於上述完全成本價格。中國有色金屬工業協會矽業分會本週釋出的資訊顯示，本週國內多晶矽市場整體陷入觀望態勢，市場成交處於停滯狀態。而在上週，國內N型復投料和顆粒矽的成交均價已經分別低至3.2萬元/噸、3.1萬元/噸。這也意味著，矽料成交價格要反彈到業內所預期的完全成本價，其反彈幅度需要超過40%。其中一位參與《倡議書》簽署的企業負責人獨家回應記者，各大企業積極落實工信部、國家市場監督管理總局近期會議精神，“大家都是自願倡議的。”該人士還進一步向記者透露，《倡議書》釋出後或還會出臺懲罰措施，加強自律。（21世紀經濟報道）

### 2026-08-07 16:26:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #Czech Republic #Foreign Exchange Reserves #Importance 0

Czech Republic Forex Reserves Rise in July — Foreign exchange reserves in the Czech Republic rose to USD 181.0 billion at the end of July 2026 from USD 180.6 billion in June. The increase was mainly driven by higher foreign currency reserves, which climbed to USD 165.5 billion from USD 164.9 billion, while gold holdings rose to USD 11.0 billion from USD 10.7 billion. The country’s IMF reserve position increased slightly to USD 0.77 billion from USD 0.77 billion, while holdings of Special Drawing Rights (SDRs) edged up to USD 3.51 billion from USD 3.49 billion. By contrast, other reserve assets fell sharply to USD 0.26 billion from USD 0.72 billion.

【國務院臺辦發言人陳斌華答記者問 (2026年8月7日)】問:民進黨當局公開揚言並封鎖大陸推出的“臺青e家”，阻止臺灣民眾訪問平臺，並稱平臺存在個資外洩風險，是大陸對臺進行輿論戰、心理戰手段。對此有何評論? 答:"臺青e家”是面向臺灣青年的公益性綜合服務平臺，旨在回應臺灣青年來大陸交流發展的需求，為臺灣青年提供交流、學習、實習、就業等全鏈條資訊服務。上線以來，得到臺灣民眾尤其是島內青年的歡迎和認可。 民進黨當局罔顧臺灣青年要交流、要發展的願望和需求，公開揚言並封鎖“臺青e家”，藉口是個資外洩、拉攏統戰、未統先治，全是"臺獨”話術。難道給臺灣青年多一個機會就叫“統戰”，讓臺灣青年自己比較就叫“風險”，實際上越封越證明“臺青e家”契合臺灣青年需求、戳中民進黨當局痛點。民進黨當局倒行逆施，下指令遮蔽網址、鎖住網頁，但鎖不住臺灣青年尋機遇、求發展的心，更封不住“臺青e家”在兩岸民眾心中的那扇家門。 大陸“十五五”起步有力、開局良好，高質量發展蘊藏廣闊機遇，是臺灣青年交流、學習、就業、創業的熱土。我們始終秉持“兩岸一家親”理念，將持續為臺灣青年來大陸築夢圓夢創造更好條件、提供更多便利。歡迎臺灣青年積極使用“臺青e家”，感受家的溫暖，體驗越來越好的服務。

China Foreign Exchange Reserves Expand — Official foreign exchange reserves kept by the People's Bank of China rose to an equivalent of $3.419 trillion in July of 2026 from $3.416 trillion in the previous month, aligned with market expectations and remaining relatively close to the record high of $3.442 trillion two weeks prior. 【Analysts: India FX reserves set to near record】Analysts say strong capital inflows driven by Reserve Bank of India measures should push India’s foreign exchange reserves close to a record within weeks. RBI data show reserves rose $10.5bn in the week to July 31 to $692.9bn, the largest weekly gain since end‑January and close to the February peak of $728.5bn. Since early June RBI actions have attracted about $40.8bn via non‑resident Indian foreign‑currency deposits and overseas borrowings; reserves have climbed for five straight weeks. Rising reserves boost the RBI’s capacity to defend the rupee, which has recovered about 1.8% from its May low.

### 2026-08-07 16:16:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #Taiwan #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Taiwan Trade Surplus Widens in July — Taiwan's trade surplus widened to USD 17.2 billion in July 2026 from USD 14.4 billion a year earlier, exceeding market expectations of USD 16.5 billion. Exports rose 32.9% year-on-year to USD 75.3 billion, supported by robust demand for machinery and equipment (37.1%), led by electronic parts (50.5%) and information, communication and audio products (29.5%). The US remained the largest export market, accounting for 31% of total shipments, with exports rising 25.2%, followed by Mainland China and Hong Kong, where exports increased 33.4%. Imports increased 37.4% to USD 58.3 billion, driven by purchases of machinery and equipment (55.8%), particularly electronic parts (61.5%) and information, communication and audio products (18.8%). Mainland China and Hong Kong remained the largest source of imports, accounting for 19.9% of the total, followed by South Korea at 13.6%. In the first seven months of 2026, Taiwan's trade surplus widened 63.5% year-on-year to USD 114.4 billion.

### 2026-08-07 16:13:28  #Trading Economics #Economy #Bulgaria #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Bulgaria Retail Sales Growth Slows in June — Retail sales in Bulgaria eased by 7.1% year-on-year in June from a 7.9% growth in the previous month. Activity slowed for automotive fuels in specialized stores (3.1% vs. 13.7%) and food, beverages, and tobacco (4.1% vs. 4.7%). Meanwhile, non-food products excluding fuel accelerated to 9.9% from 8.3%, supported by stronger sales in non-specialized stores (10.6% vs. 10.2%), mail-order and internet retail (26.4% vs. 19.1%), and textiles, clothing, and footwear (22.5% vs. 23.0%). However, growth also eased in household appliances, furniture, and other household goods (9.0% vs. 11.2%) and computer and communications equipment (8.0% vs. 4.0%), while pharmaceutical and medical goods, cosmetics, and toiletries returned to growth (1.0% vs. -2.5%). On a monthly basis, retail sales rose 0.5% in June, easing from a revised 0.7% increase in May.

FTSE MIB Edges Higher — The FTSE MIB edged higher to around 53,700 on Friday, remaining close to record levels, as markets digested fresh financial-sector earnings while assessing the next phase of industry consolidation. Monte dei Paschi rose 0.4% after reporting better-than-expected Q2 profit and evaluating strategic alternatives amid Intesa Sanpaolo’s takeover bid. Unipol reported a 42% year-on-year increase in first-half net profit, supported by strong performance in its insurance business, although its shares fell more than 1%. Larger lenders UniCredit and Intesa Sanpaolo advanced 0.5% and 0.7%, respectively. Outside the financial sector, defense stocks were among the strongest performers, with Leonardo and Avio advancing more than 2% and 5%, respectively. For the week, the benchmark index is heading for its best week in five.

【中東局勢擾動供油 日本煉廠大幅轉向美墨原油採購】受伊朗衝突引發供應中斷影響，日本煉油企業正越來越多地轉向波斯灣以外的原油來源，其中包括美國。這標誌採購策略出現重大轉變。日本最大煉油商 ENEOS 控股首席財務官田中聰一郎於週五財報會後簡報會上表示，該公司 8 月所需原油已落實，採購來源包含美國原油，同時選用避開霍爾木茲海峽的中東運輸航線；9 月原油貨輪也計劃沿用類似方式完成採購。競爭對手科斯莫能源控股則將大量中東原油替換為美國、墨西哥等國貨源，已儲備可供用到 10 月的原油。該公司高階執行官員巖井智樹週四在新聞釋出會上稱，目前其原油約半數來自北美與中美洲。上述表態印證，隨著中東衝突重塑全球原油流向，日本煉油商已調整採購策略。除霍爾木茲海峽持續面臨運輸擾動外，近幾周受伊朗支援的葉門胡塞武裝襲擊影響，經由紅海運輸的沙特原油貨運可靠性也有所下降。

### 2026-08-07 16:08:23  #其他

【拓竹首度透露3D生成模型，拆件3D列印或迎顛覆時刻】近日，基於3D生成模型的三維部件分割研究論文《SegviGen: Repurposing 3D Generative Model for Part Segmentation》入選SIGGRAPH 2026 Journal Track。值得注意的是，論文作者名單中出現了北京航空航天大學、中國人民大學、清華大學、消費級3D列印廠商Bambu Lab拓竹科技等機構。這是拓竹科技首次對外披露其3D生成大模型的研究進展，讓3D列印行業的內容生成、模型理解和可製造工作流產生了直接關聯。其出現在論文作者名單中也釋放出一個重要訊號，其技術佈局可能正從列印硬體、切片軟體和模型社群，進一步延伸到3D內容生成、結構理解和可製造模型處理等更底層環節。對於該技術是否會整合到現有軟體生態，記者向拓竹方面求證，並未取得明確答覆，其具體路線圖也暫時不清楚。但從其參與頂會研究的深度來看，學術成果向產品端遷移或將是大機率方向。（21財經）

DAX Rises to Fresh Record — The DAX 40 rose 0.6% to near 26,300 on Friday, setting a fresh record high and marking the second day of advances. Attention remained centered on corporate earnings and Middle East developments. On the data front, Germany’s industrial production and exports topped market expectations, providing a positive signal for the economy. The technology sector, particularly software companies and chipmakers, led sectoral gains, followed by pharma & healthcare and industrials, while insurers faced strong selling pressure. SAP climbed 3.4% and Infineon Technologies added 1.6%. Rheinmetall, Scout24, Qiagen and Brenntag also advanced solidly. Daimler Truck slipped nearly 3%, after reporting an 18% drop in second-quarter profit and despite raising full-year adjusted EBIT guidance. Munchener Ruck slid 2.7% even after a suprise 6% increase in net profit. Allianz fell 0.9%, despite stronger-than-expected record Q2 operating profit, as net income came in below forecasts.

1.透過電子郵件將意見傳送至：shujuju@cac.gov.cn。

意見反饋截止時間為2026年9月7日。

### 2026-08-07 15:51:19  #公司 #市場

【中歐基金杜厚良：經過近期調整 AI產業鏈中部分公司估值已處於相對低位區間】中歐基金權益研究部副總監杜厚良認為，近期科技板塊回撤主要源於海外高槓杆資金清盤，並非產業邏輯逆轉。杜厚良表示，AI是超過十年的長週期產業，從供需兩端來分析，供給端受軟硬體降本和全球產業鏈產能擴張的約束，目前與需求高速增長之間仍存在明顯缺口。杜厚良指出，經過近期調整，AI產業鏈中部分公司的估值已處於相對低位的區間。這一定程度上反映了市場情緒的悲觀，同時也需審慎看待其中的風險——低估值本身並不構成上漲的充分條件。當前，行業算力供給與token需求之間的錯配短期內難以根本緩解，景氣高位仍有基本面支撐，但需持續跟蹤訂單兌現和產能釋放節奏。 (澎湃新聞)

French Stocks at New Record High — The CAC 40 rose 0.3% to 8,722 on Friday, hitting a fresh record high as investors assessed key European economic data and monitored developments in the Middle East. Meanwhile, the trade deficit narrowed more than expected to €5.8 billion in June from €7.9 billion in May, as exports increased 2.6% month-on-month to €54.5 billion while imports fell 0.9% to €60.4 billion. Geopolitical risks remained in focus after Iranian state media reported a naval operation against "hostile targets" near the Strait of Hormuz. Tehran is also reportedly seeking to restrict access to the waterway for US and Israeli vessels and require compensation from hostile nations before granting transit. 【債券交易員嚴陣以待 非農就業資料或左右美聯儲9月加息前景】債券投資者正密切關注週五將公佈的美國勞動力市場資料，該資料可能緩和市場對美聯儲在9月下次會議上加息的日益升溫預期。利率掉期市場目前預計，美聯儲在9月加息25個基點的機率已超過50%。週四有報道稱，如果未來幾周通脹資料居高不下，美聯儲主席凱文·沃什準備加息，此後這一機率進一步上升。勞動力市場若出現疲軟跡象，可能緩解市場對就業過熱推高通脹的擔憂。美國7月消費者價格指數(CPI)和生產者價格指數(PPI)將於下週公佈，這些資料可能最終決定利率走勢。“如果你是美聯儲主席，你最希望看到的是‘金髮姑娘式’的就業資料，既不要太強，也不要太弱，”Haverford Trust投資策略主管Hank Smith表示。“今年大部分時間我們的基本判斷一直是12月加息一次，但我們承認，9月加息的機率已經有所上升。”經濟學家預計，7月新增非農就業人數約為8萬人，高於6月，但仍將處於今年較低水平。美國勞工統計局本週二公佈的資料則顯示，勞動力市場基本穩定，裁員規模仍然有限。

【宇樹科技王興興：人形機器人毛利率回落系多重原因所致】王興興今日在宇樹科技科創板IPO網上路演中表示，公司在2023年銷售了5臺初代版本H1產品，因當時商業化人形機器人較為稀缺，該批產品的銷售單價和毛利率均較高，與後續年度不具有可比性。2025年，人形機器人的毛利率有所回落，主要原因是：一方面，公司於2024年5月自研發布了G1款中型人形機器人，並快速成為人形機器人的主力銷售機型，由於G1作為中型人形機器人的單位價格、單位成本、毛利率均低於全尺寸機器人H1，帶動了公司人形機器人整體毛利率的下降。另一方面，公司持續透過生產降本和採購降本以最佳化產品單位成本，在此基礎上，公司在2025年適當下調了產品的銷售價格。資料顯示，2023年至2025年，宇樹科技人形機器人的毛利率分別為87.67%、69.26%和63.18%，呈現逐年下滑趨勢。（科創板日報）

### 2026-08-07 15:35:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #Slovakia #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Slovakia Trade Surplus Narrows in June — Slovakia posted a trade surplus of EUR 372.9 million in June 2026, narrowing from EUR 445.8 million in the same month last year as imports outpaced exports. Exports rose 8.2% year on year to EUR 10.1 billion, supported by a more than 6% increase in motor vehicle shipments. Exports to EU member states grew by over 11%, while shipments to non-EU countries edged down 0.2%. Meanwhile, imports increased 9.5% to EUR 9.7 billion, driven by a nearly 6% rise in machinery and transport equipment imports. Imports from EU countries climbed by more than 11%, while those from non-EU countries increased by 6%. For the first half of 2026, Slovakia's trade surplus widened to EUR 1.67 billion, up from EUR 1.32 billion in the corresponding period a year earlier.

India 10Y Yield Rises Ahead of Bond Sale — The yield on India’s 10-year G-Sec edged up to around 6.78% on Friday, retracing part of this week's decline as a sharp rebound in oil prices dampened demand for government bonds ahead of the New Delhi's INR 320 billion debt auction. Investors closely monitored the sale of five-year and 40-year securities for cues on demand, particularly for longer-dated debt. Meanwhile, Brent crude climbed to around $83.7 per barrel, up nearly 6% amid renewed concerns over shipping through the Strait of Hormuz, raising worries over India's import bill, inflation, and the rupee. Higher oil prices also pushed the US 10-year Treasury yield up to 4.68%, reducing the appeal of emerging-market debt. Still, expectations that the Reserve Bank of India will maintain ample banking system liquidity following its dovish policy decision and lower inflation forecast continued to underpin sentiment, while reports of RBI intervention in the foreign exchange market helped limit pressure on the rupee.

### 2026-08-07 15:21:48  #Trading Economics #Economy #France #Current Account #Importance 1

France Current Account Deficit Largest in 4 Months — France's current account deficit widened to a seasonally adjusted €1.4 billion in June 2026 from an upwardly revised €1.2 billion in the previous month. It marked the largest shortfall since February, driven by a weaker services balance, with the surplus narrowing to €5.2 billion from €6.3 billion, while the primary income surplus edged down to €3.6 billion from €3.7 billion. Partly offsetting the decline, the goods trade deficit narrowed to €5.3 billion from €6.3 billion. Within goods trade, the deficit excluding energy eased to €1.5 billion from €1.7 billion, while the energy shortfall shrank to €3.8 billion from €4.6 billion. The secondary income deficit remained unchanged at €4.9 billion. For the first half of 2026, France recorded a cumulative current account deficit of €6.5 billion.

### 2026-08-07 15:18:26  #Trading Economics #Economy #Austria #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Austria Trade Gap Hits 6-Month High — Austria’s trade deficit widened to EUR 635.5 million in May 2026 from EUR 476 million in the corresponding month of the previous year. The latest figure marked the largest trade deficit since December 2025, as import growth outpaced exports. Imports rose 1.1% year-on-year to EUR 16.3 billion, supported by stronger purchases from extra-EU countries, which increased 3.4%, while imports from intra-EU countries edged down 0.1%. Import growth was led by fuels and energy (+34.4%), processed goods (+4.7%), and machinery and vehicles (+4.4%), offsetting the decline in manufactured goods (-1.6%). Meanwhile, exports rose just 0.2% to EUR 15.7 billion, driven largely by higher shipments to extra-EU countries, which increased 1.8%, while exports to intra-EU countries fell 0.5%. Export growth was strongest in chemical products (+11.9%) and other goods (+12.5%), while shipments of machinery and vehicles declined 3.2%.

Switzerland Consumer Morale Lower than Expected — Switzerland’s consumer confidence index fell to -35 in July 2026, down from -33 a year earlier and below market expectations of -34, signaling a continued deterioration in household sentiment. Consumers became more pessimistic about their past financial situation, with the corresponding index falling to -44 from -41, while expectations for their future financial position declined to -32 from -28. Sentiment toward making major purchases also weakened significantly, dropping to -55 from -51, suggesting that households remain cautious about discretionary spending. Meanwhile, perceptions of the broader economic outlook were broadly stable, edging down only slightly to -37 from -36 a year earlier. Despite the annual deterioration, confidence improved modestly from June, when the index stood at -36.

【China issues 15th Five-Year Plan for civil aviation development】China's Civil Aviation Administration, National Development and Reform Commission and Ministry of Transport jointly released the 15th Five-Year Plan for civil aviation. The plan mandates construction of a coordinated infrastructure system with moderately forward capacity planning while avoiding excessive overbuilding, and prioritizes smart, green and integrated infrastructure. It directs simultaneous upgrading of existing assets and optimization of new capacity, accelerating infrastructure renewal and digital-intelligent transformation to enhance safety and operational sustainability and achieve first-class facilities. The plan also calls for a domestically controllable innovation support system, demand-led problem solving, safe development of low-altitude civil aviation, faster civil aviation digitalization and technological self-reliance, and integrated development of education, science and talent to cultivate new productive capacity in the sector.

【國防部：向為國鑄盾的國防科技工作者致敬 】8月7日下午，國防部新聞發言人陳曦大校就近期涉軍問題釋出訊息。 記者：據報道，國防科技大學計算機學院王戟研究員被中央宣傳部授予“時代楷模”稱號，受到全民廣泛關注、全網熱議點贊。請介紹有關情況。 陳曦：王戟研究員是我國高可信軟體領域主要開拓者，紮根軍事教學科研一線三十餘年，始終秉持立德樹人、為戰育人初心，忠實履行以戰領研、科研為戰使命。面對高位截肢、腎衰竭等重大疾病打擊，他仍以堅韌意志和樂觀精神堅守戰位、忘我奮鬥，堅定不移攻堅國防科技自主創新高地，為大國重器關鍵軟體安全可靠作出重要貢獻。眾多網友被這位“挺好的”教授所感動、所鼓勵，稱讚他“身如長戟，為國而立；胸懷赤誠，志在強軍”。 生命向前，衝鋒不止。王戟的微笑與堅守是所有國防科技工作者默默奉獻、為國鑄盾的縮影，我們向這些以身許黨許國的楷模致以崇高敬意和誠摯祝福。廣大官兵將以他們為榜樣，牢記初心使命，勠力奮鬥強軍，以嶄新政治面貌迎接建軍一百週年。

### 2026-08-07 15:07:39  #宏觀

【國防部：推動基本實現國防和軍隊現代化取得決定性進展】 8月7日下午，國防部新聞發言人陳曦大校就近期涉軍問題釋出訊息。

記者：近日，中共中央政治局就高質量推進國防和軍隊現代化進行第二十七次集體學習，習主席在主持學習時發表重要講話。請問軍隊將如何學習貫徹習主席重要講話精神？

陳曦：習主席重要講話站在時代發展和戰略全域性的高度，深刻闡述了高質量推進國防和軍隊現代化的重大意義，全面總結了新時代以來國防和軍隊現代化建設取得的偉大成就，科學回答了高質量推進國防和軍隊現代化一系列帶方向性、根本性、全域性性的重大問題，進一步感召和激勵全軍官兵以奮鬥姿態、嶄新面貌，推動基本實現國防和軍隊現代化取得決定性進展，加快把人民軍隊建成世界一流軍隊。

國防和軍隊現代化，是為實現中華民族偉大復興提供戰略支撐的現代化，是機械化資訊化智慧化融合發展的現代化，是永葆人民軍隊性質宗旨本色的現代化。新徵程上，全軍始終堅持以習近平新時代中國特色社會主義思想為指導，深入貫徹習近平強軍思想，堅持和加強黨對軍隊的絕對領導，全面落實新時代政治建軍方略；加快先進戰鬥力有效供給，逐步構建智慧化軍事體系；加強作戰能力體系整合，紮實推進練兵備戰；加強軍事治理，調動軍地各方力量，凝心聚力推動國防和軍隊現代化行穩致遠，為以中國式現代化全面推進強國建設、民族復興偉業提供堅強戰略支撐。（國防部發布）

France Forex Reserves Increase in July — France’s official reserve assets increased to €357.05 billion at the end of July 2026 from an eight-month low of €355.39 billion in June, mainly driven by an increase in foreign currency reserves. Foreign currency reserves climbed to €34.37 billion from €32.42 billion in the previous month. Meanwhile, gold reserves declined to €276.79 billion from €277.03 billion, claims on the IMF fell to €39.42 billion from €39.46 billion, and other reserve assets decreased to €6.46 billion from €6.48 billion. In July 2025, total reserve assets stood at €303.04 billion.

US Job Growth Likely Accelerated in July — The US economy likely added 80,000 jobs in July 2026, up from 57,000 in June, pointing to a modest rebound after payroll growth slowed from the stronger pace seen in the spring. The private sector is expected to have accounted for 78,000 of those gains, while government employment likely increased by 2,000. Healthcare was likely to lead job growth in July, while a rebound in leisure and hospitality payrolls was also expected. Meanwhile, the unemployment rate is expected to have remained unchanged at 4.2%, hovering near one-year lows. Average hourly earnings likely rose 0.3% from the previous month and 3.5% from a year earlier, matching June’s pace. Overall, the report is expected to point to a relatively steady labour market.

ASX 200 Logs Second Straight Weekly Rise, Up 3.2% — The ASX 200 eased 0.1% to close at 9,264 on Friday, snapping a five-session run of gains as traders booked profits after the index hit fresh highs twice this week. Weaker U.S. stock futures also weighed on sentiment amid geopolitical headwinds following reports that Iran may move to restrict “hostile” vessels in the Strait of Hormuz. Commercial services, healthcare, tech, and financials slipped, offset by resilience in non-energy minerals and manufacturing. The big four banks fell between 1.0% and 1.6%, while other notable laggards included Fortescue (-2.3%) and Qantas (-1.1%). Still, markets posted a second successive weekly gain, up 3.2%, boosted by steady buying and a rebound in Australia's June exports. In top trading partner China, exports and imports continued to rise at double-digit rates in July, though both eased from June’s pace. Traders now await next week’s Reserve Bank interest rate decision as slowing activity and sticky costs persist despite three hikes this year

### 2026-08-07 14:57:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #France #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

France Trade Deficit Narrows More Than Expected — France’s trade deficit narrowed to €5.8 billion in June 2026 from €7.9 billion in May, smaller than the expected €6.5 billion, as exports rose while imports declined. Exports rose 2.6% month-on-month to €54.5 billion, led by natural hydrocarbons and other extractive products (+22.1%), transport equipment (+3.2%), and mechanical, electrical, electronic and computer equipment (+1.3%). By destination, exports increased to the Middle East (+28.4%), Africa (+12.3%), the Americas (+3.1%) and Asia (+2.3%), while shipments to the EU fell 1.2%. Meanwhile, imports fell 0.9% to €60.4 billion, as lower purchases of transport equipment (-5.2%), natural hydrocarbons and other extractive products (-7.7%), and refined petroleum products (-3.7%) partially offset higher purchases of mechanical, electrical, electronic and computer equipment (+4.8%). By origin, imports rose from Asia (+5.1%) and the Americas (+1.2%), while declining from the EU (-0.4%), Africa (-3.0%) and the Middle East (-34.0%).

### 2026-08-07 14:56:09  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Industrial Production Mom #Importance 1

German Industrial Output Eases in June — Germany's industrial production rose 0.2% month-on-month in June 2026, slightly higher than market expectations of 0.1% but slowing from a revised 0.7% increase in May. The moderation came amid a 3.9% fall in machinery and equipment production, which partly offset a 3.6% jump in automotive production and an 8.4% surge in the manufacture of other transport equipment. Excluding energy and construction, industrial output was unchanged, as gains in consumer goods (+1.0%) and capital goods (+0.2%) offset a 0.9% decline in intermediate goods. Outside industry, energy production increased 1.9%, while construction output rose 0.1%. Production in energy-intensive industrial branches declined 1.8% from the previous month. On a less volatile three-month basis, industrial output rose 0.7% in the April–June period compared with the previous three months. Compared with a year earlier, overall industrial production fell 0.1% in June, following no change in May.

UK Natural Gas Rises Further — UK natural gas prices rose further to 140 pence per therm on Friday, recovering most of the losses recorded earlier in the week as renewed tensions in the Strait of Hormuz heightened uncertainty over the resumption of LNG flows from the Persian Gulf. Iranian state media reported that naval forces had carried out an operation against “hostile targets” near the strait following explosions heard on Qeshm Island. Tehran is also reportedly seeking to restrict US and Israeli vessels from transiting the waterway under a proposed agreement with Oman, while demanding compensation from countries it views as hostile before allowing passage. Ongoing disruptions to LNG shipments from key exporters such as Qatar have kept global supply conditions tight, intensifying competition with Asian buyers for available cargoes and complicating Europe’s efforts to rebuild gas inventories ahead of winter.

### 2026-08-07 14:46:04  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hungary #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Hungary Inflation Rate Lowest Since 2016 — The annual inflation rate in Hungary slowed to 1.2% in July 2026 from 1.7% in the previous month, marking the lowest reading since November 2016. The slowdown was largely driven by declines in prices for food products (-1.1% vs 0.2%) and household energy (-4.3% vs -2.3% in June). At the same time, price growth softened alcoholic beverages, tobacco products (2.9% vs 3.1%), clothing items (0.6% vs 1.4%), consumer durables (1.8% vs 1.9%), and other goods, including fuels (0.3% vs 1.1%). On the other hand, inflation accelerated for services, climbing 4.7% from 4% in the prior month. On a monthly basis, consumer prices fell by 0.1%, after remaining unchanged for the past two months. Meanwhile, the annual core inflation rate, which excludes volatile items such as food and energy, edged up to 1.9% in July from 2% in the preceding period.
